# Third-party licenses

This file lists the third-party crates compiled into the Mathstral app (the `mathstral` package, target `aarch64-apple-darwin`) together with their license texts. It is generated; do not edit it by hand.

To regenerate, run from the repository root:

```sh
python3 scripts/third-party-licenses.py
```

Fonts bundled in `assets/fonts/` are not Rust crates and are covered in `assets/fonts/README.md` and `assets/fonts/UPSTREAM-NOTICE`.

384 crates.
